📋 標書撮要📄 標書全文✍️ NLM·預設

這份招標文件（標書編號：`T/2026-009/F/S`）由環境及生態局（食物科）EEB(FB) 發出，旨在為降低食物中鹽和糖委員會（CRSS）採購電視及電台宣傳短片／聲帶（APIs）及相關影片製作服務 [1-3]。

以下為根據標書內容整理的詳細投標分析及項目規格：

---

① 背景同目的 (Background & Objectives)

* 背景：CRSS 自 2015 年成立以來，一直致力引遞及協調社區減鹽減糖宣傳工作 。局方在政府新聞處（ISD）協助下，推行首個以「少鹽少糖飲食習慣」為主題的電視及電台宣傳短片／聲帶（APIs）。

* 目的：

1. 提高公眾對 CRSS 及少鹽少糖宣傳活動（包括旗下各項重點計劃）的認識 。

2. 引發積極行為改變，並促進全港不同年齡層建立可持續的少鹽少糖健康飲食習慣，作為預防非傳染病及促進公眾健康的基石 。

* 出處：*Invitation Letter_API.pdf* [p.1]；*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III [p.85-86]。

---

② 目標受眾 (Target Audience)

* 標語（Slogan）及電視／電台 APIs：全港廣大公眾市民，涵蓋社區內所有年齡層及板塊 。

* 專題影片及改編短片：各項特定措施的標的受眾（例如餐廳、茶飲店、食品製造商等）。

* 出處：*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III [p.85]。

---

③ 傳意目標 / 核心訊息 (Communication Objectives & Key Messages)

1. 少鹽少糖依然美味：採納少鹽少糖（LSS）飲食是一項容易達成的健康習慣，而且絕不需要犧牲享用美味食物和飲料的樂趣 。

2. 隨時隨地皆可做到：無論係外出用餐（dining out）、叫外賣（ordering delivery food）或喺屋企煮食（cooking at home），每個人都可以做到少鹽少糖飲食 。

3. 吉祥物登場：必須於 APIs 中展現重塑／煥然一新的少鹽少糖吉祥物（「鹽少」及「糖小姐」），以最大化宣傳效果（吉祥物資料由政府提供）。

4. 展現核心計劃：API 須簡短展示 CRSS 旗下核心計劃（例如透過 QR code 或其他適當展示方式），包括 [7-9]：

* 少鹽少糖食肆計劃 (Less-Salt-and-Sugar Restaurants Scheme)

* 無糖星期五 (Less Sugar Friday campaign)

* 預包裝食品「鹽／糖」標籤計劃 ("Salt / Sugar" Label Scheme for Prepackaged Food Products)

5. 長期標語：建議一個適合 CRSS 及 LSS 運動長期使用的雙語響亮標語（impactful slogan, bilingual）。

* 出處：*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III [p.85-87]。

---

④ 影片及製作規格 (Production Specifications)

* 集數與長度：

* TV API：1 條，長度 30 秒 。

* Radio API：1 條，長度 30 秒 。

* 專題影片 (Two-minute video)：1 條，長度 2 分鐘（介紹 CRSS 各項重點措施）。

* 精華短片 (Short videos)：3 條，每條長約 10–15 秒（改編自 2 分鐘影片，重點展示個別措施）。

* 語言、字幕與配音：

* TV API：粵語（配繁體中文書面語字幕）及英文（配英文字幕）；須提供廣東話及英文旁白配音員 。

* Radio API：粵語、英文（獨白形式 monologue-type）及普通話 ；普通話配音員建議達到國家語言文字工作委員會普通話水平測試「一級乙等」或以上資歷 。

* 2分鐘影片及3條精華短片：粵語（配繁體中文書面語字幕）及英文（配英文字幕）。

* 拍攝與輸出技術規格：

* TV API 拍攝格式：首選 HDCAM, RED ONE 或同等格式；視乎創意概念亦可考慮 HDV 或單眼相機拍攝 。

* TV API 輸出格式：16:9 (HD) XDCAM HD422 at 50Mbps in MXF 及 HDCAM 。

* Radio API 音訊格式：商業電台／新城電台為 MP3，香港電台為 WAV (48kHz/16bit) [22-24]。

* 網絡無障礙版本：TV API 須額外提供符合 W3C Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) 2.2 Level AA 標準的網絡版本 。

* 出處：*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III & Annex 4, 5, 6 [p.85, 87-89, 123, 127, 130]。

---

⑤ 指定班底及陣容要求 (Designated Crew / Talent / Mascot Requirements)

* ERB 導師／學員：標書全文未有提及 ERB 導師或學員之相關要求 [3-16, 18, 26-36]。

* KOL／名人／明星 (Celebrity Involvement)：

* 標書建議邀請適當的名人／明星參與拍攝，以增加宣傳吸引力 。

* 承辦商須負責聯絡及邀請名人，並釐清及取得表演者權利（performers' rights），確保作品可在全球、任何媒體（電視、電台、網絡、公共交通多媒體、展覽等）播放及非牟利發行 。

* 承辦商須承擔名人發型、化妝、服裝、道具及交通等費用，並於建議書中說明名人的授權期（Royalty period，標書建議最少 2 至 3 年）[31-33]。

* 吉祥物 (Mascots)：

* 必須加入重塑／煥然一新的少鹽少糖吉祥物（「鹽少」及「糖小姐」）。

* 政府會向承辦商提供吉祥物之相關資料 。

* 專業製作團隊 (Production Team)：

* 承辦商須部署具經驗及資歷的專業團隊，於香港設有辦公室，並提供全套外景及室內拍攝人手、器材及交通 。

* 出處：*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III [p.86, 89-90]。

---

⑥ 創意及策略重點 / 風格要求 (Creative & Strategy Focus & Style)

* 核心風格：訊息傳達須非常清晰、準確、簡明（clear, accurate, concise），同時具備高度吸引力、衝擊力、記憶點與說服力（impact, recall, resonance）[40-43]。

* 策略重點：

* 傳遞「少鹽少糖依然美味」、「人人隨時隨地做到（堂食、外賣、自煮）」的積極健康態度 。

* 創作適合長期使用的雙語響亮標語 。

* 將吉祥物「鹽少」與「糖小姐」無縫融入劇情，增添親和力與記憶點 。

* 巧妙植入 QR code，引導觀眾深入了解 CRSS 三大核心計劃（食肆計劃、無糖星期五、標籤計劃）[7-9]。

* 鼓勵提出創新建議（Pro-innovation Proposals，如科技／高效製作手法，佔 15 分）及 ESG 建議（如環保、無障礙、社會責任等，佔 5 分）[44-46]。

* 出處：*Quotation Document_API.pdf* Part III & Annex 1 [p.85-87, 108, 113-115]。

---

⑧ 強制要求與紅線 (Mandatory Requirements & Red Lines)

1. 預算上限紅線 (Budget Cap)：

* 估算預算介乎 HK\$1,000,000 至 HK\$1,300,000 。

* ⛔ 超過 HK\$1,300,000 的報價將直接不獲考慮（Disqualified / Not considered） 。

2. 雙信封制與遞交方式 (Submission Red Lines)：

* 必須採用雙信封制：技術建議書（Technical Proposal，一式兩份）與費用建議書（Fee Proposal，一式兩份）須分別密封於獨立信封內 。

* 外信封不可印有／顯示投標公司名稱 。

* ⛔ 不接受電子副本遞交：標書未提及 soft copy 遞交，只接受紙本 。必須於 2026-09-29 中午 12:00 前親身投遞至添馬添美道 2 號政府總部東翼 2 樓 EEB(FB) 指定報價箱 。逾期或未投遞至指定箱者不獲受理 。

3. 原創性與全球永久版權 (IP Red Lines)：

* 創意概念及素材必須為原創，不得轉售或曾被其他機構購買 。

* 完成品、劇本、素材及拍攝片段之全球及永久版權（Worldwide and perpetual copyright）均歸香港特區政府所有 。

* 承辦商須自行負擔音樂版權（CASH、IFPI、HKRIA）及播放授權費用 。

4. 配音品質與免費重錄條款 (VO Pronunciation Penalty)：

* 普通話配音員建議具備國家語委普通話水平測試「一級乙等」或以上資歷 。

* 若電台因發音不準確或不當而拒播，承辦商必須免費安排重新錄音並更換派台版本 。

5. 評分機制 (Marking Scheme)：

* 評審比重為 技術 70%：價格 30% 。

* 技術分 100 分中，20 分預留予創新建議（15 分 Pro-innovation + 5 分 ESG Proposals）。

* 出處：*Invitation Letter_API.pdf* [p.1-2]；*Quotation Document_API.pdf* [p.2, 15-16, 89-91, 107, 120]。

---

TV and Radio APIs for Less-SaIt-and-Susar Dietary Habit Campaign
The Environment and Ecology Bureau (EEB), with the assistance of the
Information Services Department (ISD), will be launching a TV and a corresponding
radio Announcement in the Public Interest (API) on the theme of "LSS Dietary
Habit" to publicise a LSS dietary habit among the Hong Kong public. I am writing
to invite you to take part in our selection exercise to appoint an agency to work on
this project.
I enclose a quotation document which sets out the background, main
messages to be covered in the APIs and the target audience, along with the scope of
work for the appointed agency.
Our estimate for the project is between HK$1,000,000 and
HK$1,300,000. Quotations above HK$1,300,000 will not be considered. Station
copies of the APIs should be delivered by 21 December 2026 unless otherwise
advised by EEB. The Government will provide all the necessary support and
assistance for filming where necessary.
If you are interested in joining the pitch, please fill in the enclosed reply
slip and return it to us by 1800 hours, 11 September 2026.
A briefing session will be conducted by the EEB and the ISD for
interested bidders at 1000 hours, 15 September 2026 in Room 1715, 17/F, East
Wmg^Central Government Offices, 2 Tim Mei Avenue, Tamar, Hone Kons.
Our expectations and requirements will be fully explained at the briefing which will
be conducted in Cantonese.
Interested agencies will be required to submit their technical proposal
and fee proposal to EEB. Depending on whether shortlisting is conducted,
shortlisted agencies (or all agencies where shortlisting is not done) will be invited to
conduct a presentation on their creative ideas in front of a selection committee
comprising representatives ofEEB and the ISD.
Key dates are as follows:
Submission of technical and fee proposals : 29 September 2026
1200 hours
Presentation session : 8 October 2026
(Exact time and venue to be confirmed)

In case of blockage of the public access to the location of the quotation
box at any time between 9 am and 12 noon on 29 September 2026, the Government
will announce extension of the closing time until further notice. Following removal of
the blockage, the Government will announce the extended closing time as soon as
practicable. The above announcements will be made via press releases on the website
of Information Services Department (http://www.info.gov.hk/gia/general/todav.htm).
If you have any enquiries, please do not hesitate to contact Miss Anna
HO at 3509 7976 / 2205 2340 or Ms Carrie CHAN at 3509 8902.

(Ms Polly LAM)

ENVIRONMENT AND ECOLOGY BUREAU 
THE GOVERNMENT OF THE HONG KONG 
SPECIAL ADMINISTRATIVE REGION 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for  
Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest (APIs) 
for Less-Salt-and-Sugar (LSS) Dietary Habit Campaign 
 
(Case Number: T/2026-009/F/S)  
 
                                                               _____________________________________________________________________________________________________________________     
 
LODGING OF QUOTATION 
 
To be acceptable as a quotation, the Schedules in Part IV – Quotation 
Schedules plus Part V – Offer to be Bound of this set of quotation document 
must be properly completed.   
 
The (A): Part IV – Schedule 1 – Service Proposal, Schedule 3 – Information 
Schedule, Schedule 4 – Non-collusive Quotationing Certificate, Schedule 5 – 
Pro-innovation Proposals and ESG Proposals, Annex 2 – Information on 
Copyright, Annex 3 – Statement on Copyright, Confidentiality Indemnity, 
Insurance and Gender Mainstreaming for Quotationers, Appendix A to Terms 
of Quotation – Contact Details, Appendix B to Terms of Quotation – Pro Forma 
Sub-contractor’s Undertaking (if applicable) and Part V – Offer to be Bound 
and the (B): Part IV – Schedule 2 – Fee proposal shall be submitted in 
separate sealed envelopes with the words “Technical Proposal” and “Fee 
Proposal” clearly marked on the outside of the envelops respectively.  
These two envelopes shall then be placed together in a sealed plain 
envelope marked “Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for 
Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for 
Less-Salt-and-Sugar Dietary Habit Campaign”.  The sealed envelope 
should be addressed to the Secretary for Environment and Ecology (Attn: 
Chairman, Quotation Opening Team).  Please do not show the Quotationer’s 
name on the envelope. 
 
The quotation should be delivered by hand to the Environment and 
Ecology Bureau (EEB) (Food Branch) Quotation Box (the Specified 
Quotation Box) located at the entrance on 2/F, East Wing, Central 
Government Offices (CGO), 2 Tim Mei Avenue, Tamar, Hong Kong.  The 
Specified Quotation Box is available for use between 0800 and 1900 hours

— 第 2 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                        
Page 2 of 144 
from Monday to Friday (except public holidays).  The quotation must reach 
the stated address by 1200 hours (Hong Kong time) on 29 September 2026 
(Tuesday).   
 
In case Tropical Cyclone Warning Signal No. 8 or above is hoisted, or Black 
Rainstorm Warning Signal or “extreme conditions after super typhoons” 
announced by the Government is/are in force for any duration between 0900 
and 1200 hours on the Quotation Closing Date, the latest date and time before 
which Quotations are to be submitted will be extended to 1200 hours on the 
next working day (excluding Saturday).  All times mentioned are Hong Kong 
time.  In the event that the services are suspended at the CGO on the closing 
date, the Quotation Closing Date will be extended to the date when the CGO 
resumes normal services.  
 
Late quotations, incomplete quotations or quotations not deposited in the 
Specified Quotation Box will not be accepted.  Quotations submitted in a 
form other than in the manner described may render the quotations 
non-conforming and not be considered further.

1.2 
Unless otherwise expressly stated to the contrary, the following rules of 
interpretation shall apply: 
 
(a) 
 references to statutes or statutory provisions shall be construed as 
references to those statutes or statutory provisions as replaced, 
amended, modified or re-enacted from time to time; and shall 
include all subordinate legislation made under those statutes; 
 
(b) 
 words importing the singular shall include the plural and vice 
versa; words importing a gender shall include all other genders; 
references to any person shall include any individual, firm, body 
corporate or unincorporate (wherever established or incorporated); 
 
(c) 
 headings are inserted for ease of reference only and shall not affect 
the construction of the Quotation Document or the Contract; 
 
(d) 
 references to a document shall: 
 
(i) 
include all schedules, appendices, annexures and other 
materials attached to such document; and 
 
(ii) 
mean the same as from time to time amended or 
supplemented;

specific obligations or deemed to be fully performed by reason 
only that the specific obligations have been performed; 
 
(v) 
the expressions “public body” and “public officer” have the 
meanings given to them in the Interpretation and General Clauses 
Ordinance (Chapter 1 of the Laws of Hong Kong).  The 
expression “officer” has the meaning given to it in the Companies 
Ordinance (Chapter 622 of the Laws of Hong Kong).   
 
1.3 
Nothing in the Contract shall be taken to restrict, derogate from or 
otherwise interfere with any power or duty, or the exercise or 
performance of any power or duty conferred or imposed by or under any 
law upon the Government or any person in the services of the 
Government.

PART I 
TERMS OF QUOTATION 
 
1. 
Invitation to Quotation 
 
Quotations are invited for the provision of the Items summarised in the 
Terms of Quotation subject to and in accordance with the Quotation 
Document. 
 
 
2. 
Quotation Submission 
 
(a) 
This Quotation relates to the execution of all of the Services 
during the Contract period as specified in Part III – Project Brief. 
Any modification to the submitted Quotation considered 
necessary by the Quotationer shall be the subject of a separate 
letter accompanying the Quotation.  Figures and words shall not 
be altered or erased; any modification shall be effected by striking 
the incorrect figure or word and inserting the correct figure or 
word in manuscript above the original figure or word.  All such 
amendments shall be initialled by the Quotationer in manuscript. 
 
 
(b) 
Quotation submitted on or via electronic means will not be 
accepted.  All Quotations must be made on paper and 
properly signed.  
 
 
(c) 
The Government may not consider a Quotation (or will not 
consider an Quotationer where it is expressly so stated) if: 
 
(i) false, inaccurate or incorrect information is given in the 
Quotation; or 
 
(ii) any proposal, document or information requested in the 
Quotation Document is not furnished in full in the Quotation; 
or 
 
(iii) the quotation is not deposited in the Specified Quotation 
Box before the Quotation Closing Time. 
 
 
(d) 
By submitting a quotation, an Quotationer will be regarded to 
have agreed to all terms and conditions set out in the Quotation 
Document.

— 第 15 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                        
Page 15 of 144 
 
(e) 
In case Tropical Cyclone Warning Signal No. 8 or above is 
hoisted, or Black Rainstorm Warning Signal or “extreme 
conditions” announced by the Government is/are in force, for any 
duration between 0900 and 1200 hours on the date specified in 
the “Lodging of Quotation” section of the Quotation Document, 
the latest date and time before which Quotations are to be 
deposited in the Specified Quotation Box will be extended to 
1200 hours on the next working day (excluding Saturday). 
 
 
(f) 
In case of blockage of the public access to the location of the 
Specified Quotation Box at any time between 0900 and 1200 
hours on the Quotation Closing Date, the Government will 
announce extension of the Quotation Closing Time until further 
notice.  Following removal of the blockage, the Government will 
announce the extended quotation closing time as soon as 
practicable.  The above announcements will be made via press 
releases on the website of Information Services Department 
(http://www.info.gov.hk/gia/general/today.htm).

4. 
Assessment of Quotation 
 
(a) 
Service Proposal, Fee Proposal and Offer to be Bound should be 
duly completed.  Quotations which are partially completed 
may not be considered. 
 
 
(b) 
 
 
The assessment will be conducted on the basis of quality (70%), 
including attractiveness and quality of design, impact and recall, 
innovative suggestions (i.e. pro-innovation proposals and

— 第 17 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                        
Page 17 of 144 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
(c) 
proposals on improving environmental protection, sustainability 
(E) or governance (G) or social responsibility notably the 
employment of people with disabilities and/or rehabilitated 
persons (S), hereafter referred to as the “ESG”), past / relevant 
experience in provision of Event Management and IT Games, as 
well as the fee sought (30%).  The submission of the Fee 
Proposal (Schedule 2) and Service Proposal (Schedule 1) and the 
presentation, if required, shall be conducted without any charge to 
the Government.  All proposals submitted will not be returned. 
Please refer to Annex 1 of Part IV –Marking Scheme and 
Assessment Criteria for details of the quotation evaluation and 
assessment criteria. 
 
The Government will not be bound to select any Quotation on the 
basis of lowest fee quoted.  The Quotation which scores the 
highest total combined technical-cum-fee score will normally be 
considered for appointment.  The Government retains the 
discretion not to appoint the Quotation that scores the highest 
total score.  The Government also reserves the right to negotiate 
with the Quotationer the terms of offer, to request for 
supplementary information relevant to the Quotation submitted by 
the Quotationer for the purpose of assessment of the Quotation, 
and if deemed fit, not to accept any of the offers. 
 
 
5. 
Quotations to Remain Open 
 
(a)    A Quotation once submitted by an Quotationer will be binding on 
the Quotationer.  By submitting a Quotation, the Quotationer 
shall be deemed to have represented to the Government that it has 
done so as the principal but not as an agent of any other person. 
If the Quotationer submits a Quotation as an agent on behalf of 
another person, full disclosure must be made in the Quotation. 
 
(b)    It is an essential requirement of this Invitation to Quotation that a 
Quotation shall remain valid and open for a period of ninety (90) 
days after the Quotation Closing Date (or such other period as 
specified in the relevant provisions of the Terms of Quotation 
(Supplement) which supplement this Paragraph) (“Quotation 
Validity Period”).  
 
(c)    If an Quotationer offers in its Quotation a period that is shorter

6. 
Prices 
 
(a)   Unless otherwise provided for in the Quotation Document, the 
Quotationer shall quote the Unit Price for each Item in the Fee 
Proposal in Hong Kong dollars or the Permissible Currency.  The 
quotations shall be subject to the following deeming provisions:   
 
(i) 
the Unit Price quoted by the Quotationer for an Item 
shall be all-inclusive of all amounts chargeable for the 
performance by the Quotationer of all obligations as 
stated in the Contract for or concerning or in relation to 
such Item including without limitation, the Accepted 
Innovative Suggestions in relation to such Item; and 
where the Unit Price is payable recurrently over each 
billing period, then the foregoing shall apply to the Unit 
Price quotation for each such billing period; or  
 
(ii) where an Accepted Innovative Suggestion does not 
concern any Item, all Service Fee payable over the entire 
Contract Period to be calculated only based on the unit 
price(s) for all Items procured under the Contract shall 
include and be deemed to include all charges payable for 
such Accepted Innovative Suggestion.  No separate 
amount shall be payable for any Accepted Innovative 
Suggestion. 
 
(b) 
Prices quoted by an Quotationer shall only be shown in the Fee 
Proposal.  In accordance with the deeming provisions as set out 
in Paragraphs 6(a)(i) and (ii) above, the Quotationer shall not 
submit any separate price quotation in relation to any item covered 
by such deeming provisions including any innovative suggestions 
where it is not expressly asked to do so in the Fee Proposal.  Any 
separate price quotation submitted in contravention of the 
aforesaid restriction shall be disregarded and will not form part of 
the Contract, or otherwise the Quotation will be disqualified. 
 
(c) 
An Quotationer must quote fixed Unit Prices for all Items and the

13. Warning against Bribery 
 
(a) 
The offer of an advantage to any public officer with a view to 
influencing the award of the Contract, or as a reward for or 
otherwise on account of such award, is an offence under the 
Prevention of Bribery Ordinance (Chapter 201 of the Laws of 
Hong Kong).  Any such act committed by a Quotationer or any 
of its officers, directors, employees or agents will render its 
Quotation null and void. 
 
 
(b) 
The successful Quotationer shall inform its officers, directors, 
employees (whether permanent or temporary), agents and 
sub-contractors who are connected with the provision of the 
Services that the offering, soliciting or accepting of advantages, 
as defined in within the meaning of the Prevention of Bribery 
Ordinance (Chapter 201 of the Laws of Hong Kong) is not 
permitted.  The successful Quotationer shall also caution its 
officers, directors, employees and agents and sub-contractors 
against soliciting or accepting any advantage or entertainment 
which may impair their impartiality in relation to the execution 
performance of the Contract.

20.  Environmental Protection 
(a) Quotationers are encouraged to minimise the impact of their activities 
on the environment and to observe the general guidelines in Guidance 
Note GN-1 (Environmental Protection) at Annex C to the Terms of 
Quotation. 
 
(b) The following environment-friendly measures are recommended to 
be adopted in the preparation of documents relating to a Quotation 
and the future performance of the Contract: 
 
(i) all documents should be printed on both sides and on recycled 
paper.  Paper that exceeds 80 gsm should not be used for the text; 
 
(ii) use of plastic laminates, glossy covers or double covers should be 
avoided as far as possible.  If art board paper has to be used as 
document covers, recyclable non-glossy paper should be used; and 
 
(iii) single line spacing should be used and excessive space in the 
margins and in between paragraphs should be avoided.

GENERAL CONDITIONS OF CONTRACT 
 
1. 
Contract 
 
The Contractor shall enter into a written agreement (the Contract) with 
EEB of the Government of the Hong Kong Special Administrative 
Region.  
 
 
2. 
Assignment and Sub-contracting 
 
(a) The Contractor shall not, without the prior written consent of the 
Government, assign, transfer, sub contract or otherwise dispose of 
any of its interests, rights, benefits or obligations under the 
Contract.  The performance of the Contract by the Contractor 
shall be personal to it.  Any person purportedly appointed by the 
Contractor as agent shall be treated as sub-contractors for the 
purpose of this Clause 2 and for the whole of the Contract. 
 
 
(b) Neither the acceptance of the Quotation nor the submission of the 
sub-contractor’s 
undertaking 
signifies 
the 
Government’s 
acceptance of any sub-contracting proposal set out in the 
Quotation.  The Government reserves the power to disapprove 
any proposed sub-contracting proposal under Clause 2(a) above. 
 
 
(c) The Government may impose conditions either to be complied 
with by the Contractor and/or any proposed sub-contractors before 
giving any approval under Clause 2(a) above including without 
limitation, if not already done so, the execution of a 
sub-contractor’s undertaking by the proposed sub-contractor in 
favour of the Government in such form and substance to be 
prescribed by the Government.  Where the Government requests 
the same, a certified copy of the sub-contract shall also be 
deposited with the Government within seven (7) days upon such 
request. 
 
 
(d) The Contractor shall remain fully liable and shall not be relieved 
from any of its obligations hereunder by entering into any 
sub-contract for the performance of any part of the Contract.  The 
Contractor shall be responsible for the acts, defaults, omissions and 
neglect of any of its officers, employees, agents, sub-contractors (at 
whatever level), and employees and agents of any such

3. 
Intellectual Property Rights 
 
(a) 
The Government shall be the exclusive owner of the Materials. 
Except for the Third Party Materials, all the Intellectual Property 
Rights in the Materials shall vest in the Government immediately 
upon creation.  Subject to Clause 3(c), the Contractor warrants 
that such Materials shall be original works created, developed or 
made by or on behalf of the Contractor. 
 
 
(b) The Contractor shall not use or allow to be used directly or 
indirectly the Materials except for the performance of its 
obligations under the Contract or except with the prior written 
approval of the Government.  “Use” includes any acts restricted 
by copyright (including reproduction) set out in sections 22 to 29 
of the Copyright Ordinance (Chapter 528 of the Laws of Hong 
Kong). 
 
 
(c) If any materials of which the Intellectual Property Rights are 
owned by third parties and incorporated into the Materials or 
supplied or used by the Contractor in the performance of the 
Contract (“Third Party Materials”), the Contractor shall identify 
the Third Party Materials to the Government and keep the 
Government informed in writing of such Third Party Materials. 
The Contractor hereby grants or in case it is not empowered to 
do so, shall at its own costs and expenses procure that there will 
be granted, in favour of the Government, its authorised users, 
assigns and successors-in-title a royalty-free, non-exclusive, 
irrevocable, perpetual, worldwide and sub-licensable licence, for 
all purposes contemplated by the Contract, to use (including 
doing any of the acts restricted by copyright set out in sections 
22 to 29 of the Copyright Ordinance) the Third Party Materials. 
 
 
(d) The Contractor warrants that: 
 
 
 
(i) 
prior to the use and incorporation of the Third Party 
Materials into the Materials or in providing the Services, the 
Contractor shall have obtained from the third party 
Intellectual Property Rights owners the grant of all necessary 
clearances and licences for itself and its authorised users and 
for the Government, its authorised users, assigns and

or any other member of the Restricted Group; or 
 
(iii) such Confidential Information is or becomes public 
knowledge other than as a result of disclosure by the 
Contractor or any other member of the Restricted Group;

5. 
Duties and Conduct of the Contractor 
 
(a) The Contractor shall be responsible at its costs and expenses for 
providing, in accordance with the provisions of this Contract, the 
Services for the duration of the Contract. 
 
 
(b) The Contractor shall diligently, promptly, properly and efficiently 
provide and co-ordinate the provisions of the Services to the 
Government and comply with its duties and obligations in this 
Contract to the satisfaction of the Government; and shall use its 
best endeavours to promote the interests of, and generally act in 
good faith in relation to, the Government. 
 
 
(c) The Contractor shall ensure that at all times it has adequate staff, 
resources, tools and equipment to fulfill efficiently and properly its 
obligations under this Contract.

specifications set forth herein for the Services, any reasonable 
complaints and criticisms that may be made. 
 
 
(e) The Contractor shall comply with all laws, rules and regulations

(f) The Contractor shall promptly provide all necessary assistance and

the Services.

matters relating to the provision of the Services and the Contractor 
shall immediately take all remedial actions at its expenses which 
may reasonably be required. 
 
 
6. 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
Postponement / Rescheduling of the Delivery Services 
In case part of any delivery schedule(s) has to be cancelled due to the 
hoisting of Tropical Cyclone Warning Signal No. 8 or above or the 
announcement of Black Rainstorm Warning Signal or “extreme 
conditions after super typhoon” by the Government, or other emergency 
or unforeseeable circumstances such as outbreak of communicable 
disease of public health significance, etc. upon the request of the 
Government, the Contractor shall provide temporary storage and 
reschedule such delivery to another date(s) agreeable to the Government 
(which may be date(s) beyond the normal service period under this 
Contract) at no additional costs or expenses to the Government, unless 
otherwise accepted by the Government.

7. 
Contractor’s Staffing 
 
(a) The Contractor shall deploy a production team to perform the 
Services.  Unless otherwise agreed by the Government in writing, 
the Contractor shall during the term of this Contract maintain an 
office in Hong Kong for the purpose of carrying out the Services. 
The Contractor warrants that each member of the production team 
shall at all times be an employee of the Contractor and not of the 
Government, and that it shall be responsible for the remuneration 
and other employment benefits and rights of the production team 
under law and shall be vicariously liable for all their acts, 
omissions and defaults. 
 
 
(b) The Government reserves the right to require replacement of any 
members of the production team by giving written notice of the 
exercise of such right to the Contractor.  The Contractor shall 
arrange the replacement within fourteen (14) days (or by a date 
agreed with the Government Representative) after receipt of such 
notice.  Any proposed addition or change of core members of the 
production team initiated by the Contractor must obtain the 
Government Representative’s prior written approval and the 
Contractor shall ensure that any replacement of any core member 
of the production team shall cause minimal disturbance to the 
provision of the Services. 
 
 
(c) In the event of the death, incapacity or termination of employment 
of any member of the production team before completion of the 
Services, the Contractor shall at its own expenses and as soon as 
reasonably practicable arrange to substitute or replace the 
individual member concerned. 
 
 
(d) The Contractor shall ensure that the substitute or replacement is 
qualified in terms of relevant experience and qualifications and is 
available at the relevant time to act as such substitute or 
replacement.  The Contractor shall without delay forward 
curriculum vitae of the proposed substitute or replacement to the 
Government and shall warrant that it is substantially complete and 
accurate in all material respects.  The deployment of such 
substitute or replacement shall be subject to the Government’s 
consent.  The Government reserves the right not to approve any

9. 
Deficiencies in the Deliverables 
 
(a) In the event that any of the Deliverables shall fail to comply with 
the requirements of this Contract, or in the event that there is a 
breach of or non-compliance with any undertaking or obligation 
on the part of the Contractor to observe and perform which is 
capable of remedy, the Government may by notice in writing to 
the Contractor at any time require the Contractor to make good 
the deficiency and/or the breach at its sole costs and expenses 
within seven (7) days or such earlier or later date as may be 
stipulated by the Government in the notice.  Should the 
Contractor become aware of such deficiency and/or breach other 
than by notice from the Government, the Contractor shall make 
good such deficiency and/or breach at its sole costs and expenses 
forthwith upon becoming aware of the same. 
 
 
(b) For a Deliverable requiring rectification or re-work by the 
Contractor, the Government Representative shall co-ordinate the 
respective comments from the various Government Policy 
Bureaux and Departments, committees and their working groups, 
statutory and/or advisory bodies and forward the consolidated 
comments and requests to the Contractor. 
 
 
(c) Payments made by the Government to the Contractor shall not 
preclude the Government from exercising its rights to request the 
Contractor to make good any deficiency or breach pursuant to 
Clause 9(a) which may be discovered subsequent to the payment.

10. 
Service Fee 
 
(a) Subject to the Contractor performing the Service to the satisfaction 
of the Government and in consideration of the performance of the 
Services by the Contractor, the Government shall pay to the 
Contractor the Service Fee in accordance with Part III – Project 
Brief.  The Contractor shall not be entitled to any other payment 
unless specifically agreed with the Government in writing. 
Unless otherwise agreed in writing, payments under this Contract 
shall be made in Hong Kong dollars. 
 
 
(b) The Contractor shall invoice the Government for the payment of 
the Service Fee according to the payment schedule mentioned in 
Part III – Project Brief after satisfactory completion of the 
Deliverables.  
 
 
(c) The Service Fee shall be inclusive of all fees, costs, charges and 
disbursements incurred by the Contractor in the performance of the 
Services, including but not limited to all the costs and expenses 
incidental to the attendance of meetings, the giving of presentations 
and briefings, the license fees and/or royalties payable by the 
Contractor in connection with the permitted use of third party 
Intellectual Property Rights in the performance of the Services and 
preparation of the Deliverables; other out-of-pocket expenses, 
including all travelling expenses, air passages, local subsistence 
allowances for expatriates and others, postage, international 
telephone calls, facsimile, telex, electronic mail expenses, office 
and hotel accommodation, secretarial support, computer facilities, 
production of Deliverables and any other out-of-pocket expenses 
incurred in carrying out the Services.  The Service Fee shall 
further be inclusive of all fees, costs, charges and disbursements 
incurred by the Contractor in appointing or engaging any advisers, 
external experts or others to perform any part of the Services under 
this Contract, or to advise or assist in relation thereto. 
 
 
(d) No fee, cost, charge or disbursement whatsoever in addition to the 
Service Fee shall be charged by the Contractor or payable by the

14. 
Force Majeure 
 
(a) If the Contractor becomes aware of any matter likely to constitute a 
Force Majeure Event, the Contractor shall forthwith notify the 
Government in writing concerning such matter and provide the 
Government with all relevant information as the Government may 
request. 
 
 
(b) Within seven (7) days after the occurrence of a Force Majeure 
Event or earlier, the Contractor shall notify the Government in 
writing of the full particulars of the Force Majeure Event including 
its nature, extent, how the Force Majeure Event has and/or will 
materially prevent it from performing the Contract or such part 
thereof, and likely duration of such material prevention. 
 
 
(c) Provided the Government is satisfied with the Contractor’s claim 
of a Force Majeure Event which has materially prevented and/or 
will continue to materially prevent it from performing its 
obligations under the Contract or such part thereof, the Contract or 
such part thereof strictly to the extent of such prevention shall be 
suspended during the subsistence of such Force Majeure Event 
commencing from a date to be agreed between the Parties 
(“Suspension due to Force Majeure”).  Where the Government is 
not so satisfied about any alleged claim of a Force Majeure Event, 
there shall be no Suspension due to Force Majeure.  The 
Contractor may not allege or claim any event as a Force Majeure 
Event.  Any failure by the Contractor to perform any obligation 
under the Contract shall be treated as default and entitles the 
Government to terminate the Contract or partially terminate the 
Contract under any applicable Sub-clause of Clause 12(a) or 
Clause 12(b) of the General Conditions of Contract or partially 
terminate the Contract under Clause 12(e) of the General 
Conditions of Contract.

(d) 
 
Without prejudice to the generality of Clause 14(c) above, whilst 
the Suspension due to Force Majeure subsists: 
 
(i) 
the Contractor shall not be required to perform any part of its 
obligations under the Contract strictly to the extent it is 
materially prevented from doing so by the Force Majeure 
Event (“Affected Obligations”) but it shall use its best 
endeavours to remove or mitigate the effect of the Force 
Majeure Event on the Affected Obligations; 
 
(ii) the Government may make alternative arrangements for the 
performance of the Affected Obligations, whether by another 
person or otherwise, without compensation to the Contractor;  
 
(iii) the Contractor shall not be entitled to any payment of money 
in respect of the Affected Obligations (if any money would 
have been payable in the first place); 
 
(iv) notwithstanding anything in the Contract to the contrary, no 
compensation shall be payable by either Party to the other due 
to any losses or damage arising from the Suspension due to 
Force Majeure; and 
 
(v) the Contractor shall continue to fully and punctually perform 
and observe all of its other obligations which are not affected 
by the Force Majeure Event in full accordance with the 
requirements of the Contract including those obligations 
which are not Affected Obligations, and to that extent, all 
terms and conditions of the Contract shall continue to apply 
and be in full force and effect. 
 
 
(e) 
 
Following the issue of a notice by the Contractor under Clause 
14(a) above which has led to Suspension due to Force Majeure 
under Clause 14(c) above, the Contractor shall keep the 
Government informed once every week or at such longer 
frequency as may be allowed by the Government, and in any event 
from time to time upon the request of the Government, of: 
 
(i) 
the likely duration of the relevant Force Majeure Event and of 
its effect of materially preventing the Contractor from 
performing the Affected Obligations;

(ii) the actions taken or proposed to be taken by the Contractor to 
mitigate or minimise the effects of that Force Majeure Event

(iii) any other matters relevant to that Force Majeure Event or the 
Contractor’s performance affected by that Force Majeure

(f) 
 
As soon as the relevant Force Majeure Event has terminated or 
otherwise that the Government considers that the Mitigation 
Actions have minimised the effect of the Force Majeure Event on 
the ability of the Contractor to perform the Affected Obligations, 
the Contractor shall forthwith notify the Government, or the 
Government may on its own, after consultation with the 
Contractor, by notice in writing to the Contractor, determine the 
appropriate date for resuming the performance of the Affected 
Obligations (“Resumption Date”).  The Contractor shall 
immediately after the Force Majeure Event or with effect from 
Resumption Date as determined by the Government in the 
aforesaid manner, resume performance of the Affected Obligations 
in accordance with the terms and conditions of the Contract.  In 
the event of any disagreement between the Government and the 
Contractor on the appropriate Resumption Date, the Government’s 
decision shall be final in the absence of manifest error. 
 
 
(g) 
 
Should a Suspension due to Force Majeure subsists for more than 
thirty (30) days, the Government shall be entitled to, but is not 
obliged to, terminate the Contract (whether in whole or in part) 
pursuant to Clause 12(b) in Part II – General Conditions of 
Contract.

PART III 
PROJECT BRIEF 
 
Purpose 
 
This brief explains the background and objectives of the production of a TV 
API and a corresponding radio API for the Environment and Ecology Bureau 
(Food Branch) (EEB(FB)) for the Committee on Reduction of Salt and Sugar in 
Food (CRSS) to promote a less-salt-and-sugar (LSS) dietary habit among the 
Hong Kong public.  The scope of work expected of the contractor is also 
explained.  In brief, quotationers are invited to – 
 
 
propose an impactful slogan, bilingual, for the CRSS and the LSS 
campaign for us on a long-term basis; 
 
create and produce a 30-second TV API; 
 
create and produce a corresponding 30-second radio API; and  
 
create, produce and adopt 
(i) one two-minute video featuring all the key initiatives of CRSS; and 
(ii) three short videos repurposing the two-minute video in (i) for 
highlighting individual initiatives 
for other uses of EEB across designated advertising platforms to be 
assigned by EEB. 
 
 
Target Audience 
 
 
General public / all age groups and segments across the community in 
Hong Kong for the slogan and APIs, and the videos for targets of the 
respective initiatives (such as restaurants, tea shops, food manufacturers). 
 
 
Background 
 
Since its establishment in 2015, CRSS has been dedicated to steering and

— 第 86 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                        
Page 86 of 144 
coordinating publicity work on reducing salt and sugar intake in the community.  
CRSS intends to launch its first-ever TV and radio APIs to further raise public 
awareness of the importance of salt and sugar reduction.  The APIs will 
reinforce essential health messages, inspire positive behavioural change, and 
encourage sustainable LSS lifestyles among individuals of all age groups across 
the territory. 
 
CRSS works closely with partner bureaux and departments, including the 
Health Bureau, Education Bureau, Centre for Food Safety under the Food and 
Environmental Hygiene Department and Department of Health, to promote LSS 
dietary habits as a cornerstone of preventing non-communicable diseases and 
promoting public health.   
 
Objectives 
 
 
To enhance awareness of the CRSS and the LSS campaign including its 
various initiatives. 
 
To induce positive behavioural change and sustain healthy dietary habits 
across all age groups. 
 
 
Key Messages 
 
1. Adopting an LSS diet is an easily achievable healthy habit that does not 
sacrifice the enjoyment of delicious food and drinks. 
2. Such a healthy diet is achievable by everyone, regardless of whether they 
are dining out, ordering delivery food or cooking at home. 
3. To maximise the impact of its core messaging across all upcoming 
initiatives and projects, including the LSS mascots (鹽少 and 糖小姐)

Launch Date 
 
The TV and radio APIs should be completed within Q4 2026 and delivered to 
TV and radio stations during the same period, subject to EEB(FB)’s discretion. 
The two-minute video and the three short videos should also be completed and 
delivered to EEB within Q4 2026, via the method specified by EEB.

Services Required 
 
The contractor is required to provide the following services, materials and 
professionals for the production of the TV and radio APIs – 
 
(a) Creative and copywriting services / materials for providing the creative 
concept, scripts, slogans, storyboards, subtitles and graphic and 
photographic images / materials.  The creative services / materials 
should be the contractor’s original idea and must not have been 
purchased by other parties beforehand.  If the contractor’s creative 
services / materials are accepted in this quotation exercise, the 
awarded contractor should not re-sell the idea to other parties. 
(b) Full crew and equipment for location shooting both outdoor and indoor in 
Hong Kong (including transportation); 
(c) Off-line and on-line editing; 
(d) Cantonese and English voice-over talents for the conversations/narrations 
for the TV API.  The voice-over / conversations / narrations of the 
English TV version should be delivered in English as far as possible; 
(e) Cantonese, English* and Putonghua** voice-over talents for the radio API; 
(f) In the event that any language version of the recording of the radio API is 
rejected for broadcast / found unsatisfactory (for example, by radio stations) 
due to inaccurate or improper pronunciation after the station copies have 
been delivered and the inaccuracy / deficiency has been confirmed / vetted 
by the Official Languages Division of the Civil Service Bureau, the 
Contractor is required to arrange another recording of the same language 
radio API to rectify the inaccuracy / deficiency, and deliver the approved 
version to radio stations for replacement at no cost to EEB(FB) and CRSS. 
(g) The Contractor should be responsible for musical arrangement of the APIs 
(tailor-made/post-scored music is preferred), clearance of all copyright 
issues and obtain necessary licences at its own cost and expense for 
broadcasting anywhere within and outside Hong Kong, any occasion and 
any usage using any media, including, but not limited to, radio channels, 
TV, video walls, the Internet and multi-media advertisements on public 
transport, and at seminars, exhibitions and other public functions /

Supervision 
 
The contractor will be supervised by EEB(FB).  In the course of production, 
the contractor should cooperate with EEB(FB), and shall comply with all 
reasonable instructions of EEB(FB).  EEB(FB) reserves the right to amend the 
storyboards, scripts, slogans, subtitles and rough cuts if necessary. 
 
 
Budget 
 
Our estimate for the cost, inclusive of contractor fee, for the production of the 
TV API, Radio API, one two-minute video and three short videos is between 
HK$1,000,000 and HK$1,300,000.  Quotations exceeding HK$1,300,000 will 
not be considered.  Payment will be made upon satisfactory completion of the 
required services and delivery of all deliverables and submission of the fully 
completed “Information on Music Copyright Form” and receipt by Government 
of the contractor’s original invoice.

Annex 1 
Marking Scheme and Assessment Criteria for Proposals for  
Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio 
Announcements in the Public Interest (APIs) for 
Less-Salt-and-Sugar (LSS) Dietary Habit Campaign 
 
A two-envelope approach with a technical to price weighting of 70:30 will be 
adopted for quotation evaluation whereby price assessment will be conducted 
only after technical assessment.  All quotations will be assessed in the 
following manner. 
 
 
Stage 1 - Completeness Check on the Quotation Offers Submitted 
 
2. 
All quotations received will be checked on whether all the documents 
and information required in the Terms of Quotation have been submitted.  
Failure to submit any of the following documents on or before the Quotation 
Closing Date will render a quotation invalid and will not be considered 
further: 
 
(a) 
the Service Proposal (Schedule 1); 
(b) 
the Fee Proposal (Schedule 2);  
(c) 
the Information Schedule (Schedule 3);  
(d) 
the Non-collusive Quotationing Certificate (Schedule 4); 
(e) 
the Pro-innovation Proposals and ESG Proposals (Schedule 5); 
(f) 
the Information on Copyright (Annex 2); 
(g) 
the Statement on Copyright, Confidentiality Indemnity, Insurance and 
Gender Mainstreaming for Quotationers (Annex 3); 
(h) 
the Offer to be Bound (Part V); 
(i) 
the Appendix A to Terms of Quotation – Contact Details; and 
(j) 
Appendix B to Terms of Quotation – Pro Forma Sub-contractor’s 
Undertaking. 
 
 
Stage 2 – Compliance with Essential Requirements 
` 
3. 
A quotation which has passed Stage 1 will be checked to determine its 
compliance with all the essential requirements as set out in the Terms of 
Quotation.  A quotation which fails to meet any of the essential 
requirements will not be considered further. A quotation which passes Stage

Note 1: for Assessment Criterion (1) – Accuracy and clarity of message 
 
Marks will be given to Assessment Criterion (1) in accordance with the 
following five-grade approach:

For Assessment Criterion (1), the guidelines for assessment of grade are as 
follows: 
 
A “Very Good” grade will be given if the proposed creative idea for the API is 
very clear, accurate and concise in the delivery of message. 
 
A “Good” grade will be given if the proposed creative idea for the API is clear, 
accurate and concise in the delivery of message  
 
A “Satisfactory” grade will be given if the proposed creative idea for the API 
is generally clear, accurate and concise in the delivery of message 
 
A “Fair” grade will be given if the proposed creative idea for the API is fairly 
clear and accurate in the delivery of message; and 
 
A “Poor” grade will be given if the proposed creative idea for the API is 
unclear and clumsy or inaccurate/incorrect in the delivery of message.  
 
Note 2: for Assessment Criterion (2) Creativity in the delivery of message  
 
Marks will be given to Assessment Criterion (2) in accordance with the 
following five-grade approach:

Note 6: Marking Guidelines for Assessment Criteria (6)(a) and (6)(b) 
 
Assessment of Pro-innovation Proposals and Environmental, Social and 
Governance (ESG) Proposals (Maximum marks: 20) 
 
Service Providers are encouraged to make pro-innovation proposals in their 
proposed creative idea and propose ESG proposals as well.  20 out of the 
maximum mark of 100 marks for Stage 3 are reserved for assessing innovative 
suggestions (i.e. maximum marks of 15 for pro-innovation proposals in their 
proposed creative idea which can help improve the delivery of the API 
production services and 5 marks for ESG proposals). These marks for 
innovative suggestions will be given to pro-innovation proposals and ESG 
proposals as follows: 
 
(a) Pro-innovation proposals which are considered directly relevant to be 
effectively and practicable in improving the delivery of the API production 
services. The benefits that this type of pro-innovation proposals shall bring 
about are not pre-defined for Quotation assessment. Pro-innovation 
proposals 
are 
technological 
means/arrangements/work 
process/solutions/equipment that can enhance efficiency, effectiveness and 
productivity of the service outcome.  The emphasis is on output-based 
service delivery of which the contributions should be visible, and 
preferably be quantifiable and measurable.  They may, for example, 
involve application/adoption of new technology/inventions and/or 
innovative application of existing/matured technology that may enhance 
service. Service Providers may propose pro-innovation proposals, which 
may not necessarily be technology-related, bringing benefits in terms of the 
following: 
 
 
better quality of the API production services; 
 
enhance efficiency of the delivery of the services; 
 
facilitate the effective dissemination of government messages; and/or 
 
any other benefits that can bring positive outcome to the publicity 
campaign. 
 
Pro-innovation proposals will be assessed in comparison to how the 
Services are previously delivered under the existing contract or the 
conventional mode of service delivery adopted by the Government. 
 
(b) ESG proposals will improve environmental protection, sustainability or 
governance or social responsibility.  They may but need not be directly 
relevant to the Services but can bring about positive values or benefits to 
the Government or the public at large.  Such positive values or benefits

 
environmental protection (e.g. reduction in energy consumption, use of 
renewable energy, such as solar energy, in the execution of the contract, 
etc.); 
 
social responsibility (e.g. employment of people with disabilities and/or 
rehabilitated persons for the contract, etc); and/or 
 
governance. 
 
Marks will not be given to any pro-innovation proposal/ESG proposal which a 
Service Provider will neither be capable of nor responsible for implementation.

A suggestion that scores marks under pro-innovation proposals will not earn 
marks again under ESG proposals and vice versa.  In case a Service Provider 
specified the type of a suggestion under both pro-innovation proposals and ESG 
proposals and the Quotation Assessment Panel (QAP) considers that the same 
suggestion could earn marks under pro-innovation proposals and ESG proposals, 
it will be taken as scoring marks under pro-innovation proposals only.  Each 
suggestion will be counted once, irrespective of the number of 
improvements/positive values/benefits involved. 
 
Service Providers should propose pro-innovation proposals and ESG proposals 
and explain clearly with sufficient details on what improvements/positive 
values/benefits to which their proposed pro-innovation proposals and ESG 
proposals can bring about as well as how they are to be implemented by filling 
in the details in the schedule of pro-innovation proposals and ESG proposals (in 
the form of Schedule C to the Terms of Quotation) to facilitate Quotation 
evaluation. 
 
Apart from the schedule of pro-innovation proposals and ESG proposals 
mentioned above, Service Providers shall submit the following information for 
demonstrating the effectiveness and practicability of the pro-innovation 
proposals/ESG proposals.  Marks will not be given if the Service Providers 
only propose a concept without sufficient details.  The information that shall 
be provided by the Service Providers includes the following – 
 
⚫ if the suggestion is concerned with a kind of technology, equipment, tool, 
system, material, facility and vehicle, etc.: scope of the services 
involved, details on how to implement, specifications, catalogues, 
features, functions, quantity, coverage, locations and outcome, etc. as 
appropriate; 
⚫ if the suggestion is concerned with a kind of measure, service, scheme 
and activity, etc.: the objective, scope of the services involved, details on

locations, frequency, duration, outcome and number of target 
beneficiaries, etc. as appropriate; and 
⚫ if the suggestion is related to manpower: the objective, scope of the

Service Providers may also be requested to provide supporting documents or a 
demonstration to prove the practicability of their pro-innovation proposals/ESG 
proposals.  All proposed pro-innovation proposals/ESG proposals will be 
assessed on the basis of the information provided in the Quotation submissions 
and the factual supporting documents (e.g. test reports/certificates) provided by 
the Service Providers upon request by the Government.  The demonstration 
will not be taken into account in marking.  It only serves as a means to enable 
members of the QAP to have a better understanding of the pro-innovation 
proposals/ESG proposals proposed by the Service Providers.  During the 
demonstration, Service Providers are also not allowed to provide additional 
information not contained in their original Quotation submissions. 
 
All practicable pro-innovation proposals / ESG proposals accepted by the 
Government shall form part of the Contract. Any failure to perform such 
pro-innovation proposals/ESG proposals would be deemed a breach of the 
contractual obligation, and the Government would be entitled to take follow-up 
actions in accordance with the existing mechanism on the handling of breach of 
contractual obligations e.g. claiming damages and/or termination of the 
contract.

Stage 5 – Calculation of Combined Score 
 
8. 
The combined score of a conforming quotation will be determined by 
the following formula – 
 
 
  
Weighted Technical Score + Weighted Price Score 
 
9. 
Normally, the quotation with the highest combined score will be 
recommended for acceptance, subject to the requirement that the Government 
is satisfied that the recommended Service Provider is fully (including 
technically, commercially and financially) capable of undertaking the Contract, 
and that the recommended quotation is the most advantageous quotation to the 
Government in accordance with the quotation provisions.

Annex 3 
Statement on Copyright, Confidentiality 
Indemnity, Insurance and Gender Mainstreaming for 
Quotationers 
 
 
 
 
Bidders are requested to note the following requirements regarding 
copyright, confidentiality, indemnity, insurance and gender mainstreaming: 
 
 
Title to Data and Copyright 
 
 
In the performance of its services hereunder, the Contractor shall 
not, and shall procure that the sub-contractors shall not, infringe the copyright 
or other intellectual property rights of any publication matters, literary, artistic, 
musical work, or things and shall in any event indemnify and keep indemnified 
the Government against all actions, claims, damages, and costs which may be 
sustained by the Government resulting from any such infringement. 
 
 
The ownership, copyright and all other intellectual property rights 
in all deliverables (including the Announcement in the Public Interest (API), all 
copies, footages, shooting segments, editing leftover and negatives thereof), 
plans, models (computer models or otherwise), sketches, drawings, computer 
software, manuals, or other particulars or things prepared by the 
Contractor/Sub-contractors or received by the Contractor/Sub-contractors in the 
course of the services shall be vested in and belong to the Government. 
 
 
Without prejudice to the generality of the foregoing, the 
Government shall have the right of using all drafts, sketches, slogans, design 
and footage shot, in whole or in segments including still and moving images 
produced therefrom, for all purposes and in whatever form. The payment by 
Government for this project is inclusive of all fees, royalties and demands 
arising from such uses by the Government as described above. 
 
 
The Government has the right to present the API produced by the 
Contractor anywhere within and outside Hong Kong using any media, including, 
but not limited to, broadcasting, video walls, the Internet and multi-media 
advertisements on buses, seminars, exhibitions, roadshow and other media.  
The successful bidder will need to pay the Composers and Authors Society of 
HK (CASH); the International Federation of the Phonographic Industry (IFPI) 
and the Hong Kong Recording Industry Alliance Limited (HKRIA) (in case of 
music from CDs, VCDs, DVDs and cassettes) for the public performance right

— 第 121 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                 
Page 121 of 144  
for Government to broadcast any copyright music as part of the API at public 
functions which are already known at the time of bidding and are stated in the 
project brief.  The contractor will not be responsible for the public 
performance right at subsequent public functions.  Such functions include 
ceremonies, exhibitions, carnivals, district events, etc. 
 
 
Confidentiality 
 
 
 
 
The Contractor/Sub-contractors shall not disclose the terms and 
conditions of the Contract, or any information, report, document, plan, software, 
data or other particulars furnished by or on behalf of Government or by third 
parties in connection therewith, to any person other than a person who is 
employed or engaged by the Contractor/Sub-contractors in carrying out the 
Contract. 
 
 
 
 
The Contractor/Sub-contractors shall not without prior written 
consent of Government publish, either alone or in conjunction with any other 
person, in any newspaper, magazine, periodical, film, video or other medium, 
any article, photograph or illustration relating to the subject matter of the 
Contract. 
 
 
Indemnity 
 
 
Insofar as permitted by law, the Government shall not be liable or 
in respect of any damages or compensation under Fatal Accidents Ordinance 
(Chapter 22), the Employees’ Compensation Ordinance (Chapter 282), the 
Occupiers Liability Ordinance (Chapter 314), or at common law by or in 
consequence of any accident, injury, or death to any workman or other persons 
in the employment of the Contractor/sub-contractors and the Contractor shall 
indemnify and keep indemnified the Government against all claims, demands, 
proceedings, costs, charges and expenses whatsoever in respect thereof or in 
relation thereof. 
 
 
The Government shall not be liable or responsible for any expenses 
which may be incurred by the Contractor (including Sub-contractors) in relation 
to the damage or loss (including wear and tear) of equipment used in the 
production of the deliverables mentioned herein.

Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                 
Page 125 of 144  
 
2.3 Recording Format 
 
One file contains only one finished material. 
 
Start of file shall be first video frame of the material. 
 
End of file shall be last video frame of the material. 
 
For example: for a 30 sec API, the frame-count / time-code of the first 
frame to be 00:00:00:00, the last frame to be 00:00:29:24 
 
2.4 Colour Bar and Tone 
 
Not required. 
 
2.5 Video Characteristics 
 
When playback the file to video signal, the maximum video level of 
material with reference to line-up signals shall be 700mV an operational 
tolerance of +25mV for luma (Y) and 700mV for each color difference 
signal (Pr, Pb).Whatever the combination of luma and chrominance 
components, the signal shall not produce an R.G.B. or PAL coded gamut 
error when measured by appropriate instrument. 
 
2.6 Loudness Control Limits 
The subjective volume should be consistent with the programme material 
whilst at the same time preventing excessive loudness. Table below 
specifies the permitted normal peaks and full ranges on a peak programme 
meter.  
 
Programme Type 
Normal Peaks  
Full Range 
Highly 
Compressed 
Materials 
4.5 
2 - 5  
Slightly 
Compressed 
Materials 
5 
2 - 6

— 第 126 頁 —
Restricted (Contract) 
 
Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest for Less-Salt-and-Sugar 
Dietary Habit Campaign                                                                 
Page 126 of 144  
 
The levels specified above are related to standard peak programme meter 
readings with reference to a steady state reading of ‘4’ (BBC scale) 
corresponding to 0dBu. 
 
2.7 Audio Channels out phase issue 
 
The phase difference of the audio channel of a stereo material is not 
allowed to be inverted, or under a sound effect that caused a similar 
inverted phase. 
 
Except dialog, TV stations accept an adaptive amount of channel phase 
difference on music and sound effect. Provide that the mix down of the 
channels would not lead to an audio level drop larger than 3dB in any 500 
milliseconds period.

APPENDIX C TO THE TERMS OF QUOTATION –  
Guidance Note GN-1 
 
 
Guidance Note GN-1 
(Environmental Protection) 
 
 
 
Contractors are encouraged to minimise the impact of their activities on the 
environment and to observe the following requirements in particular: 
 
▪ Comply with all applicable legal and other requirements on 
environmental protection. 
 
▪ Control the use of materials and resources (e.g. electricity, fuel, 
chemicals, paper, etc.) to minimise their consumption and unnecessary 
wastage.  Use environment-friendly alternatives where possible during 
your daily operations. 
 
▪ Store properly all materials/chemicals to prevent any spillage and 
leakage. 
 
▪ Minimise the production of solid waste and chemical waste.  
 
▪ Ensure all solid waste is properly handled, stored and disposed of in an 
efficient and sensitive manner to avoid any spillage and leakage.  In 
particular, all chemical waste should be handled, stored and disposed of 
in accordance with the applicable legal requirements. 
 
▪ Ensure proper and regular maintenance of all vehicles used for the 
purpose of conducting business, including controlling their noise and 
emissions. 
 
▪ Minimise all air emissions and noise generation where practicable. 
 
▪ Ensure all waste water is discharged in accordance with the applicable 
legal requirements. 
 
▪ Reuse and recycle waste wherever possible.

⚡ 創意亮點✍️ Gemini Flash＋Search

⚡ 創意亮點 — EEB·Quotation Exercise for Provision of Service for Production of a TV and Radio Announcements in the Public Interest (APIs) for Less-Salt-and-Sugar (LSS) Dietary Habit Campaign（research 掘出嚟，可以做橋嘅真嘢）

1. 🏷️ 煥然一新官方吉祥物「鹽少」與「糖小姐」

- 事實：標書 Part III 第 3.2(c) 條明訂必須於 API 中加入重塑/煥然一新（revitalised）的官方吉祥物「鹽少」（Salt Less）與「糖小姐」（Miss Sugar），局方會向中標者提供相關角色資料。（出處：Quotation Document_API.pdf (Part III Project Brief)）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以將兩位重塑吉祥物化身為 AR 動態助手，喺真人飲食場景中即時進行少鹽少糖互動。

2. 🏷️ 一套四款官方預先包裝「鹽╱糖」標籤

- 事實：官方於 2017 年推出「低鹽、無鹽、低糖、無糖」四款標準化正面標籤（Front-of-pack），彩色版附有彩通色號，為全港最標準的視覺識別。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以將四款標籤化作視覺印章 UI，喺超市揀貨場景中一貼「解鎖」健康選擇。

3. 🏷️ 「少糖星期五」買茶飲即減 \$2 優惠

- 事實：CRSS 旗下「少糖星期五」運動聯同全港約 120 間茶飲店，提供逢星期五購買少糖或無糖飲品即減 \$2 的實質獎賞。（出處：Quotation Document_API.pdf / [hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以用「星期五減 \$2」作為放假前的犒賞情境觸發點，吸引打工仔實踐少糖。

4. 🏷️ 超過 1,200 間「少鹽少糖食肆」網絡

- 事實：全港已有超過 1,230 間食肆加入「少鹽少糖食肆計劃」，且市民可在飲食平台（如 OpenRice）直接搜尋少鹽少糖菜式。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以手機「尋寶地圖/定位」為視覺裝置，展現少鹽少糖美味餐廳遍佈全港街頭。

5. 💡 港人每日攝鹽超標 70% 與「隱形鹽」疑犯

- 事實：港人 15–84 歲平均每日攝鹽 8.4 克（超世衞 5 克上限達 70%），而燒賣、點心及看似不鹹的方包均為主要鈉來源。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以用「偵探搜查/隱形疑犯」視角，揭開日常看似不鹹的食物隱藏的鹽份。

6. 💡 「少·確幸」與「愈少愈 Charm」態度轉化

- 事實：官方過往 Campaign 將傳統戒口觀念扭轉為「少·確幸」及「愈少，原來愈 Charm」，強調少鹽少糖係一種時尚高雅的 Lifestyle。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以將少鹽少糖塑造為提升個人魅力與生活質素的潮流選擇。

7. 🇭🇰 茶餐廳點餐暗語與官方「走醬/汁另上」

- 事實：本地茶餐廳有「少甜、扣底」等點餐文化，而官方已將減鹽技巧標準化為「走醬、走汁、汁另上、少鹽」的普及口訣。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以將「汁另上/走醬」包裝成型格飲食密碼，展現主角懂得享受食物原味的品味。

8. 👤 外傭少鹽少糖烹飪比賽與家庭掌廚人

- 事實：CRSS 曾舉辦外傭少鹽少糖烹飪比賽，推廣外傭以薑、蒜、檸檬汁等天然食材代替鹽糖，直接改變僱主全家的飲食習慣。（出處：[hooks-research] EEB）

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以外傭與僱主的日常廚房互動為切入點，呈現天然調味帶來美味驚喜的溫情故事。

9. 🎯 Hidden Agenda（政府點解而家做）

- 事實：這條宣傳片背後的隱藏議程，很可能是政府希望在國際社會上重塑香港的正面形象，以回應近年因政治事件和疫情而受損的聲譽，並吸引人才和旅客回流。透過強調「只在香港」的獨特體驗和「亞洲國際都會」的定位，政府試圖軟性地帶出香港的活力和多元，同時也回應市民對香港未來發展的期望，為施政報告和五年規劃營造積極的社會氛圍。此外，宣傳片也可能間接回應了市民對政府宣傳手法「老餅」的批評，嘗試以更年輕、更具創意的方式與公眾溝通，例如禁毒宣傳片中運用潮語和明星效應，甚至嘗試AI技術，儘管後者有時會引起爭議。

- 💡 可以點做橋：可以將呢個深層目的織入概念，令片唔止講程序，更打中政府想傳達嘅 message。

🎙️ 簡介會撮要（NB 由錄音）

⭐ 標書冇寫、簡介會先講嘅嘢

  1. 吉祥物「鹽少」同「糖小姐」有最新優化版本，比稿提案階段先用現有版本，中標後方獲發最新檔替換。
    🎙️ 原話：「請大家喺創造嘅時候可以用住而家嗰兩隻先嘅，標之後我哋會提供最新嘅版本」
    📄 標書：Project Brief 指將提供更新版吉祥物（refreshed characters）並需於 API 中呈現，但冇說明比稿階段應使用現有版本。
    ➡️ 影響：創意團隊比稿時無需等待新吉祥物設計檔，可直接以現有吉祥物繪劃故事板（storyboard）及擬定提案。
  2. 電視 API 設有「Double shooting requirement」（雙語雙重拍攝），演員需以廣東話及英文各現場拍攝對白一次，而非單純後期配音。
    🎙️ 原話：「特別想提一提返啦，我哋新係有一個叫做 Double shooting requirement」
    📄 標書：僅列出需提供廣東話及英文版本與配音員（VO talents），冇提及需 Double shooting（真人現場雙語拍攝）。
    ➡️ 影響：現場拍攝選角及拍攝時間需大幅增加，演員必須具備雙語現場演出能力，或需安排額外現場拍攝時間。
  3. 澄清預算上限 HK\$1,300,000 為「全包」（all-inclusive），已包含聘請名人/明星（Celebrity）的所有相關費用。
    🎙️ 原話：「呢一個詞嗰個價錢係包唔包括係呢個 range 嚟？ All inclusive。」
    📄 標書：Project Brief 僅列出估算預算為 100 萬至 130 萬元，且建議邀請名人參與，但未有明言 130 萬是否包含名人酬金。
    ➡️ 影響：若提案採用名人/明星，必須將其酬金、肖像權及造型車馬等全部費用控制在 130 萬總預算以內，製作預算需更為嚴謹。
  4. 評審委員會偏好將目標受眾聚焦於較年輕群眾，因長者普遍已有飲食健康意識。
    🎙️ 原話：「我哋本身個委員會呢個委員會係想針對多啲後生啲嘅」
    📄 標書：標書列出的目標受眾為全港所有年齡層及廣大公眾（General public / all age groups），冇標明委員會偏好。
    ➡️ 影響：創作切入點、情境設定及選角應偏向年輕化與活力感，避免採用過於老氣或說教式的風格。
  5. 若邀請名人/明星（Celebrity），建議提供 1 至 2 位人選，且其個人形象絕對不可與健康飲食產生衝突。
    🎙️ 原話：「就麻煩就可以睇一至兩位，本身個 image 都係飲食係健康啦」
    📄 標書：Project Brief 建議邀請名人並建議肖像權為 2 至 3 年，但冇限制提案人選數量（1-2位）及具體形象紅線（不可常吃不健康餐廳或與健康飲食衝突）。
    ➡️ 影響：提案時名人建議集中在 1-2 位，且必須進行背景審查，確保其具備健康或正面飲食形象，避免被評審質疑與主題衝突。

簡介會撮要

會議

date：2026-09-15 10:00

who

  • 環境及生態局（EEB）代表
  • 政府新聞處（ISD）代表

重點

  • 吉祥物鹽少及糖小姐將有最新優化版本，投標創作時先採用現有版本，中標後方提供最新版本替換。
  • API 需於 2026 年聖誕節前完成製作並於電視台及電台播映，內容須一年四季皆適用（非季節性/一次性）。
  • 電視 API 設有 Double shooting requirement（雙語拍攝要求），廣東話對白完成後須以英文再錄一次。
  • 畫面須預留手語及雙語字幕位置，並嚴格分開技術建議書（Technical Proposal）與費用建議書（Fee Proposal）。
  • 如邀請名人/明星（Celebrity）參與，建議提供 1 至 2 位人選，形象須健康且無飲食衝突，建議肖像權為 2 至 3 年。
  • 預算 HK\$1,300,000 為全包（all-inclusive），包含明星/名人相關費用。
  • 2 分鐘影片及 3 條短片旨在提升少鹽少糖 awareness，需涵蓋少鹽少糖食店計劃、少糖星期五及鹽/糖標籤計劃。
  • 目標受眾希望針對較年輕群眾，因長者普遍已較關注健康飲食。

簡介會講咗而標書冇／改咗

  • 澄清預算上限 HK\$1,300,000 為全包金額（all-inclusive），已涵蓋名人/明星（Celebrity）相關費用。
  • 補充吉祥物「鹽少」及「糖小姐」將有最新優化版本（Refined version），中標後始交付最新檔入稿。

問答

  • 問：預算金額 HK\$1,300,000 是否包含 Celebrity（名人/明星）費用？
    答：係，HK\$1,300,000 為全包金額（all-inclusive）。
  • 問：旁白或 Celebrity 方向偏好年輕活力定知名度高？
    答：委員會希望針對較年輕受眾，因為長者本身已較關心飲食健康，故年輕化方向較好，但仍看整體創意發揮。
  • 問：2 分鐘影片與 3 條短片嘅內容資訊要求如何？
    答：30 秒 API 負責傳遞 key message，2 分鐘長片及 3 條短片則負責介紹具體計劃（如少鹽少糖食店、少糖星期五、標籤計劃），可獨立拍攝或由長片剪輯出來。

評審睇重

  • 30 秒內能否清晰精準傳遞核心訊息並建立品牌印象（branding）。
  • 能否有機結合並宣導少鹽少糖三大核心計劃（少鹽少糖食店計劃、少糖星期五、鹽/糖標籤計劃）。
  • Celebrity 形象是否符合健康飲食定位，且拍攝時間能否配合 12 月前出街之緊湊日程。

對出橋／交標嘅影響

  • 參照 Working schedule 確保於 2026 年聖誕節前完成製作並交付播放。
  • 畫面設計必須預留手語及雙語字幕位置。
  • 電視 API 須安排 Double shooting（廣東話及英文雙語對白錄製）。
  • 若提案包含 Celebrity，須提供 1 至 2 位人選建議及 2 至 3 年肖像權期，並確認能配合 12 月出街時程。
  • 故事板（Storyboard）等創意提案須同時提交軟複本（soft copy）及硬複本（hard copy）。

原話

  • 請大家喺創造嘅時候可以用住而家嗰兩隻先嘅，標咗之後我哋會提供最新嘅版本。
  • 呢條 API 我哋希望係一個長遠版本，即係一年四季都可以播放。
  • 我哋本身個委員會係想針對多啲後生啲嘅，因為長者已經比較關心飲食。

📊 研究（深入＋Web 整合）✍️ gemini-2.5-flash

好的，這是一份整合後的乾淨研究報告，已去重並保留所有獨有事實，分段清晰，並以繁體中文輸出。

---

香港政府環境及生態局（食物科）減鹽減糖宣傳片網上研究報告

標書編號： `T/2026-009/F/S`

發出部門： 環境及生態局（食物科）EEB(FB)

採購目的： 為降低食物中鹽和糖委員會（CRSS）採購電視及電台宣傳短片／聲帶（APIs）及相關影片製作服務。

---

1. 議題背景

* 政策演進： 減鹽減糖已由「政策宣示」走向「餐廳＋預先包裝食品＋全民生活」的實務階段。

* 餐廳計劃： 「少鹽少糖餐廳計劃」自2019年推出，參與餐廳、快餐店、茶飲店已增至「逾1,000間」，形成具規模的「健康選項生態」。

* 預先包裝食品： 「鹽／糖」標籤計劃已在預先包裝食品層面推行，鼓勵食品商自願標示較少鹽／糖的產品，並配合超市貨架及網購平台展示。

* 防控非傳染病(NCD)： 政府近年強化減鹽減糖作為防控心血管病、糖尿病、腎病等非傳染病的核心策略之一。

* 香港攝取量： 香港人鈉攝取量長期高於世衛建議；糖攝取量尤其來自飲品及加工食品。

* 政府措施： 包括公營醫療系統推健康飲食教育；學校及機構食堂推「3少」（少油、鹽、糖）菜式；透過CRSS協調部門宣傳。

* 宣傳轉向： 宣傳層面已由初期的「技術健康訊息」（解釋健康風險、標籤閱讀、建議攝取量）過渡到近3-5年的「生活化＋餐飲實務」（教市民「外出食飯點樣叫少鹽少糖」、示範餐廳／茶飲店做低鹽低糖菜式、與網上食店平台合作展示「少鹽少糖選項」）。

* 傳播渠道： 已由傳統媒體擴展至社交平台（Facebook, Instagram, YouTube）、KOL及餐飲平台合作。「Hong Kong’s Action on Salt and Sugar Reduction」定期發放短片、圖文、食譜示範，並與餐廳、營養師或KOL合作。

* 外展活動： 包括商場或街頭巡迴展覽、烹飪示範、工作坊等，以「活動＋媒體」整合推動減鹽減糖。

---

2. 關鍵事實數據

* 少鹽少糖餐廳數量： 逾1,000間（自2019年推出至今）。

* 鈉攝取量： 香港人鈉攝取量長期高於世衛建議。

* 糖攝取量來源： 主要來自飲品及加工食品。

---

3. 相關官員單位

* 環境及生態局（食物科）EEB(FB)： 招標文件發出部門。

* 降低食物中鹽和糖委員會（CRSS）： 宣傳活動的採購方，負責協調部門宣傳。

* 食物安全中心 (CFS)： 負責推動「鹽／糖」標籤計劃。

* 衞生署： 負責「有營食肆 EatSmart@restaurant.hk」及「3少」相關宣傳，並推動健康飲食教育。

---

4. 受眾 Sentiment

* 對「教訓式」官片的反感： 醫生／營養師白袍出鏡「正襟危坐講解」的標準格式，容易被香港觀眾視為「教訓式」官片。

* 對「濫用象徵」的疲勞： 「鹽樽／糖罐」直觀象徵（如桌上大量鹽撒出／糖倒滿杯、方糖堆成山）已非常普及於本地及國際健康飲食／糖飲品宣傳，缺乏新鮮感。

* 對「三代同堂家庭」情節的預期： 「三代同堂家庭晚餐＋長者健康警示」的套路（如爺爺／爸爸突然不適、醫生建議少鹽少糖）已是常見情節，缺乏新意。

---

5. 近期宣傳與競品
5.1 香港政府及相關機構宣傳

* 「Hong Kong’s Action on Salt and Sugar Reduction」(CRSS / EEB / CFS 系列宣傳)

* 形式： 網上短片、社交媒體貼文、廚師示範、營養師訪談；曾利用電視及電台節目、贊助健康飲食內容。

* 調性： 正規、偏理性健康教育；用卡通圖像、資訊圖表解釋健康風險。

* 內容重點： 提醒留意醬料、加工食品中的「隱藏鹽／糖」；教市民如何在餐廳要求少鹽少糖。

* 少鹽少糖餐廳計劃 (Less-Salt-and-Sugar Restaurants Scheme)

* 對外宣傳： 官網清單＋與OpenRice合作的「減鹽減糖餐廳」主題頁；媒體報導及社交平台 spotlight 個別參與餐廳。

* 常見 approach： 用餐廳老闆/廚師/侍應訪問形式，講述菜式改良及客人反應；展示「貼紙／標誌」貼在門口或餐牌上的視覺。

* Tone： 正面、生活化，但仍偏向「健康倡議＋政策性」；視覺上以餐廳場景、食物 close-up 為主。

* 「鹽／糖」標籤計劃 (Salt / Sugar Label Scheme for Prepackaged Food Products)

* 宣傳： 官網、食品安全通訊、商界宣傳物料，向食品商推動自願參加；教消費者認得標籤及理解其意思。

* 視覺： 標準化 icon／label 設計，配合貨架／包裝視覺。

* Tone： 信息主導、偏 technical，少情緒故事。

* 「少糖星期五」(Less Sugar Friday) 活動

* 推行形式： 以「指定日子減少糖攝取」概念，配合線上宣傳、活動。

* 常見內容： 提醒市民在星期五選擇少糖飲品／甜品；可能搭配社交媒體 hashtag 或打卡活動。

* Tone： 比一般健康宣傳較輕鬆，帶少少「生活儀式感」。

* 衞生署「有營食肆 EatSmart@restaurant.hk」及「3 少」（少油、鹽、糖）相關宣傳

* 形式： TV / online short clips、海報、餐廳貼紙、社交媒體。

* 常見 approach： 以家庭／上班族／長者作主角，展示「揀有營菜式」情境；用醫生／營養師出鏡講解影響。

* Tone： 認真、少量幽默，偏「政策＋健康教育」。

* 「健康生活」、「防控糖尿病／心血管疾病」等 API / 宣傳

* 內容： 強調少鹽少糖作為其中一個健康生活元素，配合運動、不吸煙等。

* 視覺： 醫院場景、家庭吃飯場景、跑步等「標準健康影像」。

5.2 國際競品 / 同類創意做法

* 英國 / 歐洲 (Dept of Health / NHS / Public Health England)

* 針對議題： 減鹽減糖、肥胖、糖稅。

* 常見 approach： 用「視覺化糖量」（如一杯汽水倒出多粒方糖）；用「未來健康後果」（孩子現時飲食 vs 長大後健康問題）。

* Tone： 部分曾使用 shock tactic（黑暗背景＋器官損壞圖像）；近年轉趨生活化，強調「easy swaps」(換飲品、換零食)。

* 新加坡 / 亞太 (新加坡健康促進局)

* 針對議題： 少糖飲品、飲品分級、餐飲健康選擇。

* 常見做法： 強調「飲品 label 顏色」讓你簡單選擇；用 office workers / young adults 作主角。

* Tone： 機智幽默＋強烈 call to action，推「選健康不難」。

* WHO 及國際 NGO 線上 Campaign

* 內容： 教市民計算每日鹽／糖攝取；提醒預防高血壓、糖尿病。

* 視覺： 極簡插畫、資訊圖表、動畫 short clips。

* 調性： 非常「數據＋事實」導向，情緒較少。

---

6. 香港角色

* 政府部門協調： 環境及生態局（食物科）透過降低食物中鹽和糖委員會（CRSS）協調各部門推動減鹽減糖宣傳。

* 跨平台推廣： 利用傳統媒體、社交平台、KOL及餐飲平台合作，推動減鹽減糖訊息。

* 政策與實踐結合： 將減鹽減糖從政策宣示落實到餐廳、預先包裝食品及全民生活層面。

---

官方用語對照表
1. 部門 / 辦事處

* 環境及生態局 (食物科)

* 官方英文全名：Environment and Ecology Bureau (Food Branch) (EEB(FB))

* 官方網址：[https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/index.html](https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/index.html)

* 降低食物中鹽和糖委員會

* 官方英文全名：Committee on Reduction of Salt and Sugar in Food (CRSS)

* 官方網址：[https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/index.html](https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/index.html)

* 食物環境衞生署

* 官方英文全名：Food and Environmental Hygiene Department

* 官方網址：[https://www.fehd.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html](https://www.fehd.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html)

* 食物安全中心

* 官方英文全名：Centre for Food Safety (CFS)

* 官方網址：[https://www.cfs.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html](https://www.cfs.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html)

* 衞生署

* 官方英文全名：Department of Health

* 官方網址：[https://www.dh.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html](https://www.dh.gov.hk/tc_chi/index.html)

* 醫務衞生局

* 官方英文全名：Health Bureau

* 官方網址：[https://www.healthb.gov.hk/tc/index.html](https://www.healthb.gov.hk/tc/index.html)

* 教育局

* 官方英文全名：Education Bureau

* 官方網址：[https://www.edb.gov.hk/tc/index.html](https://www.edb.gov.hk/tc/index.html)

2. 計劃 / 政策 / 程序

* 「少鹽少糖」飲食習慣運動

* 官方英文名稱：Less-Salt-and-Sugar (LSS) Dietary Habit Campaign

* 少鹽少糖餐廳計劃

* 官方英文名稱：Less-Salt-and-Sugar Restaurants Scheme

* 官方網址：[https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/restaurants.html](https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/restaurants.html)

* 少糖星期五運動

* 官方英文名稱：Less Sugar Friday campaign

* 官方網址：[https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/less_sugar_friday.html](https://www.eeb.gov.hk/food/tc/committees/crss/less_sugar_friday.html)

* 預先包裝食物「鹽/糖」標籤計劃

* 官方英文名稱：「Salt / Sugar」Label Scheme for Prepackaged Food Products

* 官方網址：[https://www.cfs.gov.hk/tc_chi/programme/programme_rdss/programme_Salt_Sugar_Label_Scheme.html](https://www.cfs.gov.hk/tc_chi/programme/programme_rdss/programme_Salt_Sugar_Label_Scheme.html)

* 非傳染病

* 官方英文名稱：Non-communicable diseases

3. 專有名詞 / 技術詞

* 電視宣傳短片

* 官方英文名稱：TV Announcement in the Public Interest (TV API)

* 電台宣傳聲帶

* 官方英文名稱：Radio Announcement in the Public Interest (Radio API)

* 宣傳標語

* 官方英文名稱：Slogan

* 吉祥物

* 官方英文名稱：Mascots

* 中文名稱：鹽少、糖小姐

* 二維碼

* 官方英文名稱：QR code

* 高清影片

* 官方英文名稱：High-definition Video (HDV)

* 繁體中文書面語

* 官方英文名稱：Traditional Chinese subtitles

4. 常見會錯譯 / 易混淆的地方

* 「少鹽少糖」

* 正確英文寫法：Less-Salt-and-Sugar (LSS)

* 避免使用 "low salt low sugar" 或 "reduced salt reduced sugar"，官方文件統一使用 "Less-Salt-and-Sugar"。

* 「委員會」

* 正確英文寫法：Committee

* 避免使用 "Council" 或 "Board"，特指「降低食物中鹽和糖委員會」時應使用 "Committee"。

* 「運動」/「計劃」

* 「少鹽少糖飲食習慣運動」應使用 "Campaign"。

* 「少鹽少糖餐廳計劃」應使用 "Scheme"。

* 「少糖星期五運動」應使用 "campaign"。

* 「預先包裝食物『鹽/糖』標籤計劃」應使用 "Scheme"。

* 需根據具體語境和官方名稱來區分使用 "Campaign" 和 "Scheme"。

* 「宣傳片」

* 在政府語境中，通常指 "Announcement in the Public Interest (API)"，而非一般的 "promotional video" 或 "advertisement"。

🏛️ 政府 JOB 鐵則
  1. 除標書表明，人物一般使用華人面孔（東亞／香港華人），唔好生成西方／白人臉
  2. 衣著連戲：同一角色每一格都著返同一套衫（衫款／顏色／髮型／配件固定），除非故事明寫佢換咗衫
  3. 全 board 畫風完全一致：同一 render 風格／同一打燈／同一色調／同一線條筆觸，由頭到尾統一，似同一個畫師一次過畫
  4. Storyboard 盡量唔好出現雨傘；若劇情一定要有雨傘，唔可以係黃色
🔧 Optional 餵 AI（於 Debriefing 階段隨機餵 AI）

唔強制，諗橋時隨機抽嚟畀 agency 撞火花。

💭 5W1H Provocation✍️ Gemini 2.5 Pro·預設

5W1H 創意 Provocation — EEB

WHO — 對邊個講 + 佢而家點

- Q1 如果呢個 message 只可以打動「最唔關心、最 cynical」嗰個觀眾 —— 要加咩一個 element(畫面/聲/人物/情境)先 3 秒內 hook 住佢、令佢唔 skip?

→ 加入極具食慾感嘅「日常熱氣騰騰美食特寫」（如茶餐廳爆汁堂食或外賣鑊氣），配合 1 秒極速剪接與臨場 ASMR 咀嚼/鑊氣音效。首 3 秒以視覺與聽覺直接衝擊，瞬間打破觀眾對「少鹽少糖 = 枯燥清淡」嘅預設偏見。

- Q2 呢班受眾平時食開咩視聽語言(平台/節奏/語氣)?要 adapt 邊種佢熟悉嘅風格,先入到腦而唔似「政府嘢」?

→ 受眾日常習慣 Social Media 短影音（如 Reel / TikTok）嘅快節奏視聽語言（前 3 秒爆點、高頻剪接、滿版視覺標籤）。要 adapt 廣受大眾歡迎嘅「美食開箱 / 飲食 Vlog」紀實質感與第一人稱視角，配合廣東話口語旁白，甩掉傳統政府宣傳片嘅說教感。

WHAT — 收窄到一個 + 證據

- Q3 成個 campaign 只准觀眾記住一個 element(一個畫面 / 一個 visual device / 一句)—— 你揀咩?點解係佢?

→ 揀重塑後嘅官方吉祥物「鹽少」與「糖小姐」視覺符號。因為吉祥物係標書指定必出嘅核心資產（Mandatory asset），最容易轉化為跨平台、跨年齡層嘅具體視覺標籤，並能順暢連結「少鹽少糖餐廳計劃」等核心政策。

- Q4 客戶 key message 好抽象,要用咩具體 element(真場面/真數字/真人/真物)去「show 唔 tell」,令佢落地、唔流口號?

→ 用「堂食（dining out）、外賣（delivery）、自煮（home cooking）三大真實生活情境」嘅實景鏡頭，搭配「少鹽少糖標籤」或「食肆二維碼 QR code」實物視覺元素，直觀證明少鹽少糖隨手可得且美味不減。

WHY — 點解信 + 情感

- Q5 觀眾點解要信 / 關佢事?要加咩證據 element 或情感共鳴 element,先由「事不關己」變「同我有關」?

→ 加入「全港平民日常最常食嘅餐點（如外賣飯盒、茶飲少糖選擇）」情感共鳴元素，配合食客享用美食時真實滿足嘅表情特寫，將「政府健康政策」切實連結到觀眾「每日每一餐嘅切身美味體驗」。

WHEN / WHERE — 出街情境

- Q6 呢條嘢實際喺邊度、咩狀態被睇到(電視 prime / 車廂無聲 / 手機 scroll / 戶外經過)?要 adapt 咩風格(有聲冇聲 / 首 3 秒 / 大字 / 節奏)先食到當下情境?

→ 條片會喺電視 Prime Time、公共交通車廂及手機 Social 多渠道播放，必須 adapt 「無聲勝有聲」嘅雙語動態大字 Super 視覺風格。首 3 秒以高對比配色與醒目字體呈現，確保車廂靜音或手機 scroll 時無聲亦能精準傳達訊息。

- Q7 出街時間(事件前/中/後)decide 調子係「預告期待」定「總結成就」?要加咩 element 配合呢個時間感?

→ 出街時間為 2026 年第四季（Q4 2026），調子屬於引發全民生活新習慣嘅「行動起跑期」。要加入「全城參與 / 無糖星期五」嘅節奏感元素，配合亮麗明快嘅調色與音效，營造「隨時隨地，由今日起輕鬆加入」嘅行動力。

HOW — 風格 + 差異化

- Q8 同類(同部門/同題材)政府片嘅「安全做法」係咩?要 adapt / 升級邊種風格 element(質感/節奏/transition/配樂/美術流派),先做到「框內最高質」而唔悶?

→ 同類政府片嘅安全做法係「枯燥圖表 + 權威旁白」。要升級為「動態電影級調色（Cinematic color grading）+ 電池級動態字體（Dynamic kinetic typography）+ 專屬輕快電音/現場音效（Tailor-made music）」，讓 30 秒 TV API 達到商業廣告品質。

- Q9 要加咩一個 unexpected element(反差/新 visual device/新手法),先同部門過往 campaign 拉開距離、唔似翻炒,但又唔踩紅線?

→ 加入「真人實景與 2D/3D 重塑吉祥物（鹽少、糖小姐）跨次元互動」嘅視覺對比元素（Unexpected visual device），打破過往政宣片單一真人或單一動畫格式，既拉開與過往舊 campaign 距離，又完全符合標書對吉祥物與高傳意力嘅要求。

---

*用法:agency / 委員會落橋前先消化呢 9 條,順住 element + 風格方向諗,唔好離地。*
